- **&lt;app&gt; Verified Status**: Emits a status table for &lt;app&gt; where each row states what works, how it was verified, and what was not verified or run; never claims a live run that did not happen. ([Facta-Dev/beautifulgo](https://github.com/Facta-Dev/beautifulgo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/verified-status) · ★1)
- **&lt;commit-change&gt;**: Use when the user asks to commit the current work, make a commit, or save the staged changes as a commit. Stages named paths, has &lt;commit-drafter&gt; draft the message from the staged diff, and commits only through &lt;runner command&gt; so the hooks always run. ([mnaimfaizy/agent-kit](https://github.com/mnaimfaizy/agent-kit/tree/HEAD/examples/patterns/commit-pipeline) · ★1)
- **&lt;competition-slug&gt;**: &lt;コンペ名&gt; のコンテキスト。&lt;タスク種別・ドメイン&gt;。概要/評価/データ/公開解法/実験ログ/運用ランブックのインデックス。 ([jum-matsukuma/my_template_repo](https://github.com/jum-matsukuma/my_template_repo/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/templates/competition))
- **&lt;create-pull-request&gt;**: Use when the user asks to open, create or publish a pull request for the current branch. Has &lt;pr-drafter&gt; draft the title, labels, merge base and body from the branch, then opens or updates the pull request only through &lt;runner command&gt;. Requests no reviewer unless the user names one. ([mnaimfaizy/agent-kit](https://github.com/mnaimfaizy/agent-kit/tree/HEAD/examples/patterns/pr-pipeline) · ★1)
- **&lt;directory-name&gt;**: Use when &lt;concrete trigger&gt;; use &lt;nearest-neighbour&gt; instead for &lt;excluded trigger&gt;. Produces &lt;named result&gt; for &lt;consumer&gt;. ([peterbamuhigire/chwezi-research-engine](https://github.com/peterbamuhigire/chwezi-research-engine/tree/HEAD/templates/skill-template) · ★3)
- **&lt;directory-name&gt;**: &lt;One sentence, third person: what it does + when to use it. Injected into Hermes's Level-0 metadata list — write it for the agent deciding whether to load you. Max ~200 chars.&gt; ([iammatthias/styx](https://github.com/iammatthias/styx/tree/HEAD/souls/_template) · ★1)
- **&lt;directory-name&gt;**: &lt;One sentence, third person: what it does and when to use it. Injected into Hermes's Level-0 metadata — write it the way an agent reads it when deciding whether to load. Max ~200 chars.&gt; ([iammatthias/styx](https://github.com/iammatthias/styx/tree/HEAD/skills/_template) · ★1)
- **&lt;domain&gt;-&lt;object&gt;**: &lt;Verb&gt; the &lt;artifact&gt; … Use when &lt;specific trigger phrases&gt;. Do NOT use for &lt;adjacent case handled by another skill&gt;. ([muhammaddadu/ai-skill-collection](https://github.com/muhammaddadu/ai-skill-collection/tree/HEAD/TEMPLATE) · ★1)
- **&lt;external-skills&gt;**: Use when asked to add, refresh, change or remove a skill written outside this repository, or when &lt;check command&gt; fails. Installs only through &lt;installer&gt;, at project scope, and keeps &lt;path to approved-external-skills.md&gt; and &lt;path to skills-lock.json&gt; in step. ([mnaimfaizy/agent-kit](https://github.com/mnaimfaizy/agent-kit/tree/HEAD/examples/patterns/external-skills-registry) · ★1)
- **&lt;folder-name-lowercase-hyphens&gt;**: &lt;What it does. Use when the user asks for &lt;X&gt; or mentions &lt;Y&gt;.&gt; ([razorvision/clara-skills](https://github.com/razorvision/clara-skills/tree/HEAD/templates/minimal-skill))
- **&lt;folder-name-lowercase-hyphens&gt;**: &lt;What the skill does, in third person, one or two sentences. Then the trigger: Use when the user asks for &lt;X&gt;, mentions &lt;Y&gt;, or needs &lt;Z&gt;.&gt; ([razorvision/clara-skills](https://github.com/razorvision/clara-skills/tree/HEAD/templates/full-skill))
- **&lt;folder-name&gt;**: &lt;What it does, in one clause&gt;. Use when &lt;the phrases and situations a user actually brings, including ones that don't name the skill&gt;. Do NOT use for &lt;the nearest sibling cases, naming the skill that owns each&gt;. ([sadpandajoe/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/sadpandajoe/ai-toolkit/tree/HEAD/docs/templates) · ★1)
- **&lt;hook-name&gt;**: &lt;What this skill does. Keep under 1024 chars.&gt; ([HugoLopes45/aiclair](https://github.com/HugoLopes45/aiclair/tree/HEAD/packages/hooks/_template) · ★3)
- **&lt;Human-readable role name&gt;**: One paragraph: what the role does and when it triggers. ([mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin](https://github.com/mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin/tree/HEAD/src/skills/residential_multifamily/roles/_TEMPLATE) · ★52)
- **&lt;Human-readable workflow name&gt;**: One paragraph: what the workflow does and when it triggers. ([mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin](https://github.com/mariourquia/cre-skills-plugin/tree/HEAD/src/skills/residential_multifamily/workflows/_TEMPLATE) · ★52)
- **&lt;job-slug&gt;**: &lt;Written for triggering: what job this performs, for which outcome, touching which systems, and the phrasings a user would actually say. - **&lt;kebab-case 技能名&gt;**: &lt;何时触发 + 做什么。写得具体、略"推"一点以利触发。不要写"用于 XX 任务"这种废描述。&gt; ([qihangzhang-272/agent-skill-library](https://github.com/qihangzhang-272/agent-skill-library/tree/HEAD/archive/legacy-plugin-layout/plugins/foundation/skills/skill-architecture/assets/skill-template) · ★2)
- **&lt;kebab-case, equals folder name&gt;**: Use when &lt;trigger in one sentence&gt;. &lt;What it produces, in one sentence.&gt; (2 sentences at most; "Use when" is required. - **&lt;kebab-case, ≤ 64 ký tự, ví dụ adding-module&gt;**: Kích hoạt khi &lt;từ khoá người dùng hay nói, tín hiệu cụ thể&gt; trong repo &lt;tên&gt;. &lt;Kết quả đầu ra, ngôi thứ ba, ≤ 450 ký tự; không tóm tắt các bước làm việc bên trong để tránh agent bỏ qua đọc thân file.&gt; ([huongmh14/writing-spec-for-ai](https://github.com/huongmh14/writing-spec-for-ai/tree/HEAD/templates/project-skill))
- **&lt;kebab-case-name-matching-folder&gt;**: One-line trigger that another agent will read to decide whether to invoke this skill. Lead with the verb, name the artifact. ([evolplus/talos](https://github.com/evolplus/talos/tree/HEAD/skills/_template) · ★9)
- **&lt;kebab-case-name&gt;**: &lt;one-sentence trigger condition — when should this skill be loaded? Start with "Apply when ..." for consistency.&gt; ([sordi-ai/skill-everything](https://github.com/sordi-ai/skill-everything/tree/HEAD/skills/_template) · ★21)
- **&lt;kebab-case-name&gt;**: &lt;This line decides whether the skill ever gets read — it matters more than the body. - **&lt;kebab-case-nombre&gt;**: &lt;Una frase clara con los TRIGGERS — "Usar cuando ...". - **&lt;kebab-case-skill-name&gt;**: &lt;一句话说明这个 Skill 做什么、什么时候触发。写得"主动"一些—— 涵盖用户可能的多种表述方式，包括直接调用和隐含需求的场景。 非空、≤1024 字符。这是触发机制的主要依据，注册脚本会读它写进 substage-skill-map.yaml。 所有"何时使用"的信息放这里，**不放正文**（不要写 "## When to use" 章节）。 ([pdl-openair/OpenAIR](https://github.com/pdl-openair/OpenAIR/tree/HEAD/skills/_template) · ★16)
- **&lt;kebab-case-skill-name&gt;**: &lt;One sentence — what the skill does + when to use it. Keep under ~280 chars. - **&lt;kebab-case-skill-slug&gt;**: &lt;one sentence — the procedural / framing gap this skill closes for *this class of work* (e.g., "reviewing corporate authorization documents for a credit-facility closing across multi-entity, multi-jurisdiction guarantor groups"). Do not mention specific dollar amounts, percentages, named entities, verbatim quotes, or jurisdiction- / statute- / entity-type-specific citations that you only know to mention because you read *this* scenario's documents. The description should read like the title of a CLE module, not the abstract of a single matter.&gt; ([sunyifeisb-art/legalwork](https://github.com/sunyifeisb-art/legalwork/tree/HEAD/skills/awesome-legal-aiagent-skills/_template) · ★67)
- **&lt;kebab-case-slug&gt;**: &lt;What this skill does. Use this whenever the user mentions &lt;concrete contexts and trigger phrases&gt;, even if they don't explicitly ask for &lt;skill name&gt;.&gt; ([labs21-dev/agents-stack](https://github.com/labs21-dev/agents-stack/tree/HEAD/skill-creator/templates/skill-skeleton) · ★27)
- **&lt;kebab-case-slug&gt;**: &lt;What this skill does, in one sentence. Then the activation triggers: "Activates when &lt;task shapes, file types, or user phrasings that should fire this skill&gt;." The description is the ONLY signal harnesses use to auto-activate — write it for the router, not the reader.&gt; ([Neogenuity/harness-kit](https://github.com/Neogenuity/harness-kit/tree/HEAD/plugins/harness-kit/skills/harness-kit/templates/docs/skills/_example))

- **&lt;kebab-name&gt;**: &lt;≤300 chars, third-person, behavioral, names what + when (trigger phrases)&gt; ([fabioc-aloha/Alex_ACT_Edition](https://github.com/fabioc-aloha/Alex_ACT_Edition/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/skill-creator/assets/skill-skeleton))
- **&lt;kebab-name&gt;**: &lt;one paragraph: what this playbook does AND when to reach for it — the description doubles as the when-to-use trigger.&gt; ([Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim](https://github.com/Infernal-Crack-LED/nikke-sim/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/_TEMPLATE))

- **&lt;layer&gt;-engineering-standards**: GENERATED ONCE PER LAYER/LANGUAGE of your chain by /adapt-to-stack, into the repo's own .claude/skills/ under a per-layer directory name (e.g. schema-standards, backend-standards, frontend-standards). The coding + review standard for &lt;LAYER/LANGUAGE&gt;. Loaded when writing or reviewing &lt;LANGUAGE&gt; code, or when running as the &lt;layer&gt;-specialist agent. Deliberately carries NO rules for other layers so it stays cheap to load. - **&lt;layer&gt;-write-core**: GENERATED ONCE PER LAYER of your chain by /adapt-to-stack, into the repo's own .claude/skills/ beside &lt;layer&gt;-standards. The short write-time rule set for &lt;LAYER/LANGUAGE&gt;: only the rules that are expensive to get wrong once the code compiles. Preloaded by &lt;layer&gt;-specialist and slice-&lt;layer&gt;-specialist through their skills: line, so it is in context for every edit. The full &lt;layer&gt;-standards is for review, not for writing. ([konstantinos-malavazos/claude-code-playbook](https://github.com/konstantinos-malavazos/claude-code-playbook/tree/HEAD/templates/skills/write-core) · ★3)

- **&lt;merge-train&gt;**: Use when the user asks to merge a batch of pull requests, or asks in what order to merge them.
